Title: Chronicon Ephratense, enthaltend den Lebens-Lauf des ehrwürdigen Vaters in Christo Friedsam Gottrecht, weyland Stiffters und Vorstehers des geistlichen Ordens der Einsamen in Ephrata in der Graffschaft Lancaster in Pennsylvania
Creator: Brother Lamech (d. 1763) and Agrippa (Johann Peter Miller, 1710-1796), of the Ephrata community
Publisher: Ephrata, Pa.: Druck der Brüderschaft
Publication Date: 1786
Rights: Public Domain (published 1786, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Language: deu
Source: Internet Archive item spohn1131 (https://archive.org/details/spohn1131), the item's own spohn1131_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Elizabethtown College; host metadata date: 1786. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-16. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: b0ff7dc126414f6e58a3fc2c5371323ba44be217e7fb59d31d6eb2854b666401.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; 1557 characters of archive.org page chrome and pre-title library noise before the marker 'Chronicon Ephratente,' removed; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. The file as fetched opened with an inserted 1955 Lutheran newspaper obituary clipping (about 70 lines) that is not part of the book; it was removed up to the title page's own first line. The scan reads the German Fraktur with the long s kept as the character itself and with frequent f/s and E/F misreads ("Finfamen" for "Einsamen").
Content note: Original German of the Ephrata chronicle, printed on the community's own press in 1786; the English translation of 1889 is vendored beside it (lamech-agrippa_chronicon-ephratense_hark1889.txt). Primary original-language witness; usable for locating and checking passages, with every quotation re-read against the page image.
